`io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client.VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.InputStreamWriteStream`, but it is not resuming from a wrong thread, it is rather caused by the fact that `InputStreamWriteStream` is subject to the following awkward sequence of calls:\n\n1. `InputStreamWriteStream.close()` from `com.sun.xml.fastinfoset.Decoder.closeIfRequired()`\n2. `InputStreamWriteStream.read(byte[], int, int)` from `HTTPConduit.close()` (with the intention to \"pull off the unread data     (like closing tags that we didn't need to read)\")\n3. And `InputStreamWriteStream.close()` from `com.sun.xml.fastinfoset.Decoder.closeIfRequired()` again.\n\nThe problem is that the first `InputStreamWriteStream.close()` sets `InputStreamWriteStream.readBuffer` to null and therefore the subsequent `InputStreamWriteStream.read(byte[], int, int)` call may, in some cases, wait forever for new data in InputStreamWriteStream.queue`.\n\nThe solution is not to set `InputStreamWriteStream.readBuffer = null` if it is set to an `ErrorBuffer` meaning there is an error or the stream is at the end which we signal through the `END` constant also being of type `ErrorBuffer`.\n\nOriginal report:\n\n```\nError:  Tests run: 2, Failures: 0, Errors: 1, Skipped: 0, Time elapsed: 37.73 s \u003c\u003c\u003c   <div data-target="react-app.reactRoot"><link rel="preload" as="image" href="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/1826249?u=a1afb52c18daa60c4976e1468faabb6db6a81a9c&amp;v=4&amp;size=48"/><link rel="preload" as="image" href="https://avatars.githubusercontent.com/u/1826249?s=64&amp;u=a1afb52c18daa60c4976e1468faabb6db6a81a9c&amp;v=4"/><meta name="react-app-name" content="issues"/><a hidden="" id="code-view-repo-link" href="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf" data-discover="true"></a><!--$--><!--$--><script type="application/ld+json">{"@context":"https://schema.org","@type":"DiscussionForumPosting","headline":"`FastInfosetTest.fastInfoset` fails intemittently","articleBody":"Edit 2025-01-30: This turns up to be an issue independent from https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1700.\n\nThe cause is also in `io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client.VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.InputStreamWriteStream`, but it is not resuming from a wrong thread, it is rather caused by the fact that `InputStreamWriteStream` is subject to the following awkward sequence of calls:\n\n1. `InputStreamWriteStream.close()` from `com.sun.xml.fastinfoset.Decoder.closeIfRequired()`\n2. `InputStreamWriteStream.read(byte[], int, int)` from `HTTPConduit.close()` (with the intention to \"pull off the unread data     (like closing tags that we didn't need to read)\")\n3. And `InputStreamWriteStream.close()` from `com.sun.xml.fastinfoset.Decoder.closeIfRequired()` again.\n\nThe problem is that the first `InputStreamWriteStream.close()` sets `InputStreamWriteStream.readBuffer` to null and therefore the subsequent `InputStreamWriteStream.read(byte[], int, int)` call may, in some cases, wait forever for new data in InputStreamWriteStream.queue`.\n\nThe solution is not to set `InputStreamWriteStream.readBuffer = null` if it is set to an `ErrorBuffer` meaning there is an error or the stream is at the end which we signal through the `END` constant also being of type `ErrorBuffer`.\n\nOriginal report:\n\n```\nError:  Tests run: 2, Failures: 0, Errors: 1, Skipped: 0, Time elapsed: 37.73 s \u003c\u003c\u003c data-component="Link" href="https://github.com/ppalaga" data-testid="issue-body-header-author">ppalaga</a></div><div class="footer__7ecx1m footerSection__kzv2bq"><span>opened </span><a class="dateLink__IC75fj prc-Link-Link-9ZwDx" data-component="Link" href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1636#issue-2709127440" data-testid="issue-body-header-link"><relative-time aria-haspopup="true" aria-expanded="false" tabindex="0" class="prc-Button-ButtonBase-9n-Xk prc-Button-IconButton-fyge7" data-loading="false" data-no-visuals="true" data-size="small" data-variant="invisible" aria-labelledby="_R_fqlmkjmr9mm5_" id="_R_qlmkjmr9mm5_"><svg data-component="Octicon" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false" class="octicon octicon-kebab-horizontal" viewBox="0 data-team-hovercards-enabled="true" class="markdown-body" data-turbolinks="false"><div class="markdown-body safe-html-box__Uwc6Al Container__ZAxOBo"><p dir="auto">Edit 2025-01-30: This turns up to be an issue independent from <a class="issue-link js-issue-link" data-error-text="Failed to load title" data-id="2820498642" data-permission-text="Title is private" data-url="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1700" data-hovercard-type="issue" data-hovercard-url="/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1700/hovercard" href="https://github.com/quarkiverse/quarkus-cxf/issues/1700">#1700</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">The cause is also in <code class="notranslate">io.quarkiverse.cxf.vertx.http.client.VertxHttpClientHTTPConduit.InputStreamWriteStream</code>, but it is not resuming from a wrong thread, it is rather caused by the fact that <code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream</code> is subject to the following awkward sequence of calls:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream.close()</code> from <code class="notranslate">com.sun.xml.fastinfoset.Decoder.closeIfRequired()</code></li>
<li><code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream.read(byte[], int, int)</code> from <code class="notranslate">HTTPConduit.close()</code> (with the intention to "pull off the unread data     (like closing tags that we didn't need to read)")</li>
<li>And <code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream.close()</code> from <code class="notranslate">com.sun.xml.fastinfoset.Decoder.closeIfRequired()</code> again.</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">The problem is that the first <code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream.close()</code> sets <code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream.readBuffer</code> to null and therefore the subsequent <code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream.read(byte[], int, int)</code> call may, in some cases, wait forever for new data in InputStreamWriteStream.queue`.</p>
<p dir="auto">The solution is not to set <code class="notranslate">InputStreamWriteStream.readBuffer = null</code> if it is set to an <code class="notranslate">ErrorBuffer</code> meaning there is an error or the stream is at the end which we signal through the <code class="notranslate">END</code> constant also being of type <code class="notranslate">ErrorBuffer</code>.</p>
<p dir="auto">Original report:</p>
<div class="snippet-clipboard-content notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="Error:  Tests run: 2, Failures: 0, Errors: 1, Skipped: 0, Time elapsed: 37.73 s &lt;&lt;&lt; FAILURE! -- in io.quarkiverse.cxf.it.fastinfoset.FastInfosetTest
